{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01LU9xgUPTnALvMy4c7iZXks","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01GQzeFXprq3FnTYdmkkmyoc","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"gpu-6000pro-2","command":"cat > /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/scripts/detector_baselines/run_all_detectors.py << 'PYEOF'\n\"\"\"\nDetection-based baselines for recursive generation depth estimation.\n\nComputes 5 continuous detection scores on 3x3 grid data, then evaluates\nordinal depth prediction via LR, RF, and quantile binning.\n\nDetectors:\n  1. Binoculars: CE_observer / CE_performer (gpt2-xl / pythia-1.4b)\n  2. Fast-DetectGPT: normalized (token_ll + entropy) discrepancy\n  3. Log-Rank: average log-rank of actual tokens under scoring model\n  4. Entropy: average predictive entropy\n  5. LLR: log-likelihood ratio (ll_observer - ll_performer)\n\nEvaluation:\n  - 5-fold CV Logistic Regression (per-score + combined)\n  - 5-fold CV Random Forest (per-score + combined)\n  - Quantile binning\n  - Pairwise AUC: d0v1, d1v2, d2v3\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\nfrom sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, roc_auc_score\n\nPROJECT_ROOT = \"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\"\n\nGRID_CELLS = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\": \"data/\",\n    \"pythia_temp09\": \"data/pythia_temp09/\",\n    \"pythia_topk50\": \"data/pythia_topk50/\",\n    \"olmo_nucleus\": \"data/olmo/\",\n    \"olmo_temp09\": \"data/olmo_temp09/\",\n    \"olmo_topk50\": \"data/olmo_topk50/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_nucleus\": \"data/gpt2xl/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_temp09\": \"data/gpt2xl_temp09/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_topk50\": \"data/gpt2xl_topk50/\",\n}\n\nMODEL_PATHS = {\n    \"gpt2xl\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/gpt2-xl\",\n    \"pythia\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\",\n}\n\nNUM_DEPTHS = 4\nMAX_SEQ_LEN = 512\n\n\nclass TextDataset(Dataset):\n    def __init__(self, texts, tokenizer, max_len=512):\n        self.encodings = tokenizer(\n            texts, max_length=max_len, truncation=True,\n            padding=\"max_length\", return_tensors=\"pt\"\n        )\n\n    def __len__(self):\n        return self.encodings[\"input_ids\"].shape[0]\n\n    def __getitem__(self, idx):\n        return {k: v[idx] for k, v in self.encodings.items()}\n\n\ndef load_cell_data(data_dir, max_per_depth=5000):\n    texts, labels = [], []\n    for d in range(NUM_DEPTHS):\n        fpath = os.path.join(data_dir, f\"depth_{d}.jsonl\")\n        if not os.path.exists(fpath):\n            print(f\"  WARNING: {fpath} not found, skipping depth {d}\")\n            continue\n        count = 0\n        with open(fpath) as f:\n            for line in f:\n                line = line.strip()\n                if not line:\n                    continue\n                rec = json.loads(line)\n                texts.append(rec[\"text\"])\n                labels.append(d)\n                count += 1\n                if count >= max_per_depth:\n                    break\n        print(f\"  depth_{d}: {count} samples\")\n    return texts, np.array(labels)\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef compute_all_scores(model, tokenizer, texts, device, batch_size=8, label=\"model\"):\n    dataset = TextDataset(texts, tokenizer, MAX_SEQ_LEN)\n    loader = DataLoader(dataset, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=False)\n\n    all_ll, all_entropy, all_logrank, all_fdgpt = [], [], [], []\n\n    for i, batch in enumerate(loader):\n        input_ids = batch[\"input_ids\"].to(device)\n        attention_mask = batch[\"attention_mask\"].to(device)\n\n        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\n        logits = outputs.logits[:, :-1, :]\n        targets = input_ids[:, 1:]\n        mask = attention_mask[:, 1:].float()\n        lengths = mask.sum(dim=1).clamp(min=1)\n\n        log_probs = torch.log_softmax(logits, dim=-1)\n        probs = torch.softmax(logits, dim=-1)\n\n        # Log-likelihood\n        token_ll = log_probs.gather(2, targets.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)\n        token_ll_masked = token_ll * mask\n        mean_ll = token_ll_masked.sum(dim=1) / lengths\n        all_ll.append(mean_ll.cpu().numpy())\n\n        # Entropy\n        entropy = -(probs * log_probs).sum(dim=-1)\n        entropy_masked = entropy * mask\n        mean_entropy = entropy_masked.sum(dim=1) / lengths\n        all_entropy.append(mean_entropy.cpu().numpy())\n\n        # Log-Rank\n        target_probs = probs.gather(2, targets.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)\n        ranks = (probs > target_probs.unsqueeze(-1)).sum(dim=-1).float() + 1.0\n        log_ranks = torch.log(ranks)\n        log_ranks_masked = log_ranks * mask\n        mean_logrank = log_ranks_masked.sum(dim=1) / lengths\n        all_logrank.append(mean_logrank.cpu().numpy())\n\n        # Fast-DetectGPT\n        discrepancy = token_ll + entropy\n        discrepancy_masked = discrepancy * mask\n        mean_d = discrepancy_masked.sum(dim=1) / lengths\n        sum_sq = ((discrepancy - mean_d.unsqueeze(1)) ** 2 * mask).sum(dim=1)\n        std_d = (sum_sq / lengths.clamp(min=2)).sqrt().clamp(min=1e-8)\n        fdgpt_score = mean_d / std_d\n        all_fdgpt.append(fdgpt_score.cpu().numpy())\n\n        if (i + 1) % 50 == 0:\n            print(f\"    [{label}] batch {i+1}/{len(loader)}\")\n\n    return {\n        \"ll\": np.concatenate(all_ll),\n        \"entropy\": np.concatenate(all_entropy),\n        \"logrank\": np.concatenate(all_logrank),\n        \"fdgpt\": np.concatenate(all_fdgpt),\n    }\n\n\ndef quantile_binning(scores, labels, n_bins=4):\n    thresholds = np.quantile(scores, [i / n_bins for i in range(1, n_bins)])\n    preds = np.digitize(scores, thresholds)\n    return accuracy_score(labels, preds)\n\n\ndef cv_evaluate(X, labels, clf_factory, n_splits=5, seed=42):\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_splits, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\n    accs = []\n    oof_preds = np.zeros_like(labels)\n\n    for train_idx, test_idx in skf.split(X, labels):\n        clf = clf_factory()\n        clf.fit(X[train_idx], labels[train_idx])\n        preds = clf.predict(X[test_idx])\n        oof_preds[test_idx] = preds\n        accs.append(accuracy_score(labels[test_idx], preds))\n\n    return {\n        \"acc_mean\": round(float(np.mean(accs)), 4),\n        \"acc_std\": round(float(np.std(accs)), 4),\n    }\n\n\ndef pairwise_auc(scores, labels, d_low, d_high):\n    mask = (labels == d_low) | (labels == d_high)\n    if mask.sum() < 10:\n        return None\n    y = (labels[mask] == d_high).astype(int)\n    s = scores[mask]\n    try:\n        auc = roc_auc_score(y, s)\n        return round(float(max(auc, 1 - auc)), 4)\n    except ValueError:\n        return None\n\n\ndef score_stats(scores, labels):\n    return {\n        f\"d{d}\": {\"mean\": round(float(scores[labels == d].mean()), 4),\n                   \"std\": round(float(scores[labels == d].std()), 4)}\n        for d in range(NUM_DEPTHS) if (labels == d).sum() > 0\n    }\n\n\ndef evaluate_single_score(name, scores, labels):\n    X = scores.reshape(-1, 1)\n\n    lr_result = cv_evaluate(\n        X, labels,\n        lambda: LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, random_state=42)\n    )\n    rf_result = cv_evaluate(\n        X, labels,\n        lambda: RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=100, random_state=42, n_jobs=-1)\n    )\n    bin_acc = quantile_binning(scores, labels)\n\n    aucs = {\n        \"d0v1\": pairwise_auc(scores, labels, 0, 1),\n        \"d1v2\": pairwise_auc(scores, labels, 1, 2),\n        \"d2v3\": pairwise_auc(scores, labels, 2, 3),\n    }\n\n    return {\n        \"lr_acc_mean\": lr_result[\"acc_mean\"],\n        \"lr_acc_std\": lr_result[\"acc_std\"],\n        \"rf_acc_mean\": rf_result[\"acc_mean\"],\n        \"rf_acc_std\": rf_result[\"acc_std\"],\n        \"binning_acc\": round(float(bin_acc), 4),\n        \"pairwise_auc\": aucs,\n        \"score_stats\": score_stats(scores, labels),\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_cell_scores(cell_name, data_dir, model_obs, model_perf,\n                        tok_obs, tok_perf, device, batch_size, cache_dir):\n    \"\"\"Compute raw scores for one cell, with disk caching.\"\"\"\n    cache_path = os.path.join(cache_dir, f\"{cell_name}_scores.npz\")\n    if os.path.exists(cache_path):\n        print(f\"\\n  Loading cached scores for {cell_name}\")\n        data = np.load(cache_path)\n        return {k: data[k] for k in data.files}\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"  Computing scores: {cell_name}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n    texts, labels = load_cell_data(data_dir)\n    if len(texts) == 0:\n        return None\n    print(f\"  Total: {len(texts)} samples, depths: {np.bincount(labels).tolist()}\")\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    print(\"  Scoring with observer (gpt2-xl)...\")\n    obs_scores = compute_all_scores(model_obs, tok_obs, texts, device, batch_size, \"gpt2xl\")\n    print(f\"    Done in {time.time()-t0:.1f}s\")\n\n    t1 = time.time()\n    print(\"  Scoring with performer (pythia-1.4b)...\")\n    perf_scores = compute_all_scores(model_perf, tok_perf, texts, device, batch_size, \"pythia\")\n    print(f\"    Done in {time.time()-t1:.1f}s\")\n\n    ce_obs = -obs_scores[\"ll\"]\n    ce_perf = -perf_scores[\"ll\"]\n\n    result = {\n        \"labels\": labels,\n        \"binoculars\": ce_obs / np.clip(ce_perf, 1e-8, None),\n        \"fast_detectgpt\": obs_scores[\"fdgpt\"],\n        \"log_rank\": obs_scores[\"logrank\"],\n        \"entropy\": obs_scores[\"entropy\"],\n        \"llr\": obs_scores[\"ll\"] - perf_scores[\"ll\"],\n    }\n\n    np.savez(cache_path, **result)\n    print(f\"  Cached to {cache_path}\")\n    return result\n\n\ndef evaluate_cell(cell_name, scores_dict):\n    \"\"\"Run all evaluations on precomputed scores.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\n  Evaluating: {cell_name}\")\n    labels = scores_dict[\"labels\"]\n    detectors = [\"binoculars\", \"fast_detectgpt\", \"log_rank\", \"entropy\", \"llr\"]\n\n    results = {\"cell\": cell_name, \"n_samples\": len(labels)}\n\n    for det_name in detectors:\n        det_scores = scores_dict[det_name]\n        print(f\"    {det_name}...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n        results[det_name] = evaluate_single_score(det_name, det_scores, labels)\n        print(f\"LR={results[det_name]['lr_acc_mean']:.4f} RF={results[det_name]['rf_acc_mean']:.4f}\")\n\n    # Combined 5 features\n    X_all = np.column_stack([scores_dict[d] for d in detectors])\n    print(f\"    combined_5feat...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n    lr_comb = cv_evaluate(X_all, labels,\n                          lambda: LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, random_state=42))\n    rf_comb = cv_evaluate(X_all, labels,\n                          lambda: RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=200, random_state=42, n_jobs=-1))\n    results[\"combined_5feat\"] = {\n        \"lr_acc_mean\": lr_comb[\"acc_mean\"], \"lr_acc_std\": lr_comb[\"acc_std\"],\n        \"rf_acc_mean\": rf_comb[\"acc_mean\"], \"rf_acc_std\": rf_comb[\"acc_std\"],\n    }\n    print(f\"LR={lr_comb['acc_mean']:.4f} RF={rf_comb['acc_mean']:.4f}\")\n\n    # Combined 3 core detectors\n    X_3det = np.column_stack([scores_dict[\"binoculars\"], scores_dict[\"fast_detectgpt\"],\n                               scores_dict[\"log_rank\"]])\n    print(f\"    combined_3det...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n    lr_3 = cv_evaluate(X_3det, labels,\n                       lambda: LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, random_state=42))\n    rf_3 = cv_evaluate(X_3det, labels,\n                       lambda: RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=200, random_state=42, n_jobs=-1))\n    results[\"combined_3det\"] = {\n        \"lr_acc_mean\": lr_3[\"acc_mean\"], \"lr_acc_std\": lr_3[\"acc_std\"],\n        \"rf_acc_mean\": rf_3[\"acc_mean\"], \"rf_acc_std\": rf_3[\"acc_std\"],\n    }\n    print(f\"LR={lr_3['acc_mean']:.4f} RF={rf_3['acc_mean']:.4f}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=0)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--batch_size\", type=int, default=8)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--pilot\", action=\"store_true\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--eval_only\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Skip GPU scoring, use cached scores\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", default=os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, \"results/detector_baselines\"))\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    cache_dir = os.path.join(args.output_dir, \"score_cache\")\n    os.makedirs(cache_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    cells = {\"pythia_nucleus\": GRID_CELLS[\"pythia_nucleus\"]} if args.pilot else       torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n    else:\n        gpu_elapsed = 0\n\n    # Evaluation phase (CPU only)\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"  EVALUATION PHASE\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    all_results = {}\n    for cell_name in cells:\n        cache_path = os.path.join(cache_dir, f\"{cell_name}_scores.npz\")\n        if not os.path.exists(cache_path):\n            print(f\"  WARNING: no cached scores for {cell_name}, skipping\")\n            continue\n        data = np.load(cache_path)\n        scores_dict = {k: data[k] for k in data.files}\n        all_results[cell_name] = evaluate_cell(cell_name, scores_dict)\n\n    rf_reference = {\n        \"pythia_nucleus\": 0.7813, \"pythia_temp09\": 0.7677, \"pythia_topk50\": 0.687,\n        \"olmo_nucleus\": 0.7877, \"olmo_temp09\": 0.827, \"olmo_topk50\": 0.737,\n        \"gpt2xl_nucleus\": 0.8397, \"gpt2xl_temp09\": 0.8527, \"gpt2xl_topk50\": 0.754,\n    }\n\n    detectors = [\"binoculars\", \"fast_detectgpt\", \"log_rank\", \"entropy\", \"llr\"]\n\n    # Comparison table\n    print(f\"\\n\\n{'='*120}\")\n    print(\"  COMPARISON: 4-class Accuracy (best of LR/RF)\")\n    print(f\"{'='*120}\")\n    header = f\"  {'Cell':<18} {'RF-19f':<9}\"\n    for d in detectors:\n        header += f\" {d[:8]:<9}\"\n    header += f\" {'Comb5':<9} {'Comb3':<9}\"\n    print(header)\n    print(f\"  {'-'*18} \" + f\"{'-'*9} \" * (len(detectors) + 3))\n\n    for cell_name in cells:\n        if cell_name not in all_results:\n            continue\n        r = all_results[cell_name]\n        rf = rf_reference.get(cell_name, 0)\n        row = f\"  {cell_name:<18} {rf:<9.4f}\"\n        for d in detectors:\n            best = max(r[d][\"lr_acc_mean\"], r[d][\"rf_acc_mean\"])\n            row += f\" {best:<9.4f}\"\n        c5 = max(r[\"combined_5feat\"][\"lr_acc_mean\"], r[\"combined_5feat\"][\"rf_acc_mean\"])\n        c3 = max(r[\"combined_3det\"][\"lr_acc_mean\"], r[\"combined_3det\"][\"rf_acc_mean\"])\n        row += f\" {c5:<9.4f} {c3:<9.4f}\"\n        print(row)\n\n    # AUC table\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*100}\")\n    print(\"  PAIRWISE AUC\")\n    print(f\"{'='*100}\")\n    for pair in [\"d0v1\", \"d1v2\", \"d2v3\"]:\n        print(f\"\\n  {pair}:\")\n        row = f\"  {'Cell':<18}\"\n        for d in detectors:\n            row += f\" {d[:8]:<9}\"\n        print(row)\n        for cell_name in cells:\n            if cell_name not in all_results:\n                continue\n            r = all_results[cell_name]\n            row = f\"  {cell_name:<18}\"\n            for d in detectors:\n                v = r[d][\"pairwise_auc\"].get(pair)\n                row += f\" {v if v else 'N/A':<9}\"\n            print(row)\n\n    # Save summary\n    summary = {\n        \"experiment\": \"detector_baselines_v2\",\n        \"detectors\": detectors,\n        \"models\": {\"observer\": \"gpt2-xl\", \"performer\": \"pythia-1.4b\"},\n for l, r in zip(lr_accs, rf_accs)]\n        means[d] = {\n            \"mean_lr_acc\": round(float(np.mean(lr_accs)), 4),\n            \"mean_rf_acc\": round(float(np.mean(rf_accs)), 4),\n {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.\n\nSummary:\n1. Primary Request and Intent:\n   The user requested implementing AI text detection methods (Binoculars, Fast-DetectGPT, DetectLLM/Log-Rank) as ordinal depth estimation baselines for a recursive generation depth estimation project. The goal is to compute continuous detection scores on a 3×3 grid (3 models × 3 decoding strategies × 4 depths) and evaluate whether these scores can predict recursive generation depth. Even negative results (detectors can't do depth estimation well) are valuable. The task specifies: scripts to `scripts/detector_baselines/`, results to `results/detector_baselines/`, use only cuda:0, and verify at least pythia_nucleus has complete results.\n\n2. Key Technical Concepts:\n   - Recursive generation depth estimation (d0=original, d1-d3=recursive generations)\n   - AI text detection methods: Binoculars (cross-perplexity ratio), Fast-DetectGPT (normalized discrepancy), Log-Rank (average log-rank of tokens), Entropy, LLR\n   - Ordinal depth classification: 4-class (d0-d3) using LR, RF, quantile binning\n   - Pairwise AUC metrics: d0v1, d1v2, d2v3\n   - Two reference models: gpt2-xl (observer) and pythia-1.4b (performer)\n   - Score caching to disk (npz) to separate GPU inference from CPU evaluation\n   - 19-feature RF baseline achieving 70-85% across cells\n\n3. Files and Code Sections:\n   - `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/scripts/detection_baselines.py` (368 lines, pre-existing)\n     - Contains Binoculars + Fast-DetectGPT only, missing Log-Rank/AUC/RF evaluation\n     - Was not run (results directory empty)\n     - Not modified; new script created instead\n\n   - `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/scripts/detector_baselines/run_all_detectors.py` (NEW, final version)\n     - Core script implementing all 5 detectors with full evaluation suite\n     - Key features: disk caching (`--eval_only`), `--pilot` mode, unbuffered output support\n     - Full final script content written via `cat > ... << 'PYEOF'`:\n\n```python\n\"\"\"\nDetection-based baselines for recursive generation depth estimation.\nComputes 5 continuous detection scores on 3x3 grid data, then evaluates\nordinal depth prediction via LR, RF, and quantile binning.\nDetectors:\n  1. Binoculars: CE_observer / CE_performer (gpt2-xl / pythia-1.4b)\n  2. Fast-DetectGPT: normalized (token_ll + entropy) discrepancy\n  3. Log-Rank: average log-rank of actual tokens under scoring model\n  4. Entropy: average predictive entropy\n  5. LLR: log-likelihood ratio (ll_observer - ll_performer)\n\"\"\"\n# Key constants:\nPROJECT_ROOT = \"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\"\nGRID_CELLS = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\": \"data/\",\n    \"pythia_temp09\": \"data/pythia_temp09/\",\n    \"pythia_topk50\": \"data/pythia_topk50/\",\n    \"olmo_nucleus\": \"data/olmo/\",\n    \"olmo_temp09\": \"data/olmo_temp09/\",\n    \"olmo_topk50\": \"data/olmo_topk50/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_nucleus\": \"data/gpt2xl/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_temp09\": \"data/gpt2xl_temp09/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_topk50\": \"data/gpt2xl_topk50/\",\n}\nMODEL_PATHS = {\n    \"gpt2xl\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/gpt2-xl\",\n    \"pythia\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\",\n}\n# Key function: compute_all_scores() - single pass computes ll, entropy, logrank, fdgpt\n# Key function: evaluate_single_score() - LR CV, RF CV, quantile binning, pairwise AUC\n# Key function: compute_cell_scores() - with disk caching to score_cache/*.npz\n# Key function: evaluate_cell() - runs all evaluations on precomputed scores\n# Combined evaluations: combined_5feat (all 5 scores) and combined_3det (bino+fdgpt+logrank)\n```\n\n   - Results directory: `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/results/detector_baselines/`\n     - `score_cache/` - npz files with precomputed scores per cell\n     - `summary_v2.json` - pilot results (will be overwritten by full run)\n     - `pilot_log2.txt` - pilot run log\n     - `full_log.txt` - full 9-cell run log (in progress)\n\n4. Errors and fixes:\n   - **`multi_class=\"multinomial\"` TypeError**: scikit-learn 1.8.0 removed this parameter from LogisticRegression. Fixed by removing the parameter entirely with sed, then cleaning up trailing commas.\n   - **`torch_dtype` deprecation warning**: transformers now uses `dtype` instead. Fixed in the rewritten script by changing `torch_dtype=torch.float16` to `dtype=torch.float16`.\n   - **Python output buffering**: First nohup run showed no progress because Python buffers stdout when redirected to file. Fixed by adding `-u` flag: `python -u scripts/...`.\n   - **Duplicate processes**: First run attempt timed out but left a zombie process competing for GPU memory (22GB VRAM used). Killed with `kill 718445 718450`.\n   - **LR convergence warning**: Combined 5-feature LR on unscaled features triggers ConvergenceWarning. Not blocking; results still produced (LR=72.2%). Could improve with StandardScaler but not critical.\n\n5. Problem Solving:\n   - Discovered existing `detection_baselines.py` lacked Log-Rank, AUC metrics, and RF evaluation\n   - Created comprehensive new script with 5 detectors + full evaluation suite\n   - Added disk caching to avoid re-running expensive GPU inference\n   - Pilot validated successfully on pythia_nucleus with meaningful results\n   - Log-Rank emerged as best single detector (60.1% LR accuracy, 0.908 d0v1 AUC)\n   - Fast-DetectGPT shows numerical instability (d1 mean=28515) - needs investigation\n   - Full 9-cell run is in progress (4/9 cells completed)\n\n6. All user messages:\n   - First message: Detailed task specification for Binoculars + Fast-DetectGPT + DetectLLM as ordinal depth baselines, with server info, data paths, method steps, evaluation methods, output locations, and error handling guidelines.\n   - \"CRITICAL: Respond with TEXT ONLY...\" - Current summary request.\n\n7. Pending Tasks:\n   - Full 9-cell run is in progress (4/9 complete: pythia_nucleus, pythia_temp09, pythia_topk50, olmo_nucleus). Remaining: olmo_temp09, olmo_topk50, gpt2xl_nucleus, gpt2xl_temp09, gpt2xl_topk50.\n   - After full run completes: verify results file completeness, check summary_v2.json\n   - Register artifacts with `register_artifact`\n   - Investigate Fast-DetectGPT numerical instability (d1 mean=28515 in pilot)\n\n8. Current Work:\n   The full 9-cell detector baselines run is executing on gpu-6000pro-2 as a background nohup process (PID 726550).
